<p class="flow-note">Sides are the reporting dealer&rsquo;s, as FINRA disseminates them: &ldquo;dealer bought&rdquo; means the dealer reporting the trade bought; for a trade with a customer, a dealer sale is a customer purchase. FINRA disseminates inter-dealer trades on the sell side only, so the split is not a measure of investor buying versus selling. TRACE caps reported size at $5MM+ for investment-grade bonds; capped trades are counted at the cap, so all volumes are minimums. Counts reflect the tape&rsquo;s current state: cancelled trades are excluded, and corrected trades are counted once, at their corrected values.</p>

<script>
document.querySelectorAll('.flow-panel').forEach(function(p){
  var btns = p.querySelectorAll('.flow-toggle button');
  btns.forEach(function(btn){
    btn.addEventListener('click', function(){
      btns.forEach(function(x){ x.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'false'); });
      btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
      p.querySelectorAll('.flow-win').forEach(function(w){
        w.hidden = w.getAttribute('data-w') !== btn.getAttribute('data-w');
      });
    });
  });
});
</script>

<script>
(function(){
  var cbs = document.querySelectorAll('.tape-filter input[type=checkbox]');
  cbs.forEach(function(cb){ cb.addEventListener('change', function(){
    var showT = document.getElementById('f-trades').checked;
    var showM = document.getElementById('f-marks').checked;
    document.querySelectorAll('table.tape tbody tr').forEach(function(r){
      var s = r.getAttribute('data-src');
      r.style.display = (s === 't' && showT) || (s === 'm' && showM) ? '' : 'none';
    });
  }); });
})();
</script>
